  </strong> <p class="text-body-sm multiline-text" data-v-63470d6f>Project ID: OF1308
Job Title: Senior Data Engineer – Java, GCP BigQuery &amp; Terraform

Work Schedule:

Days: Monday to Friday

Hours: 3 hours/day

Timings: 8 PM to 12 AM IST

Location: Remote via Zoom (Screen share required)

Salary: ₹30,000 per month

Role Type: Data Engineering – Cloud &amp; Infrastructure

Who Can Do This Role:
A senior data engineer with strong experience in Java-based backend development, GCP data platforms, and infrastructure as code using Terraform. The ideal candidate should be comfortable working with BigQuery for data processing, building scalable data solutions, and managing cloud infrastructure in GCP environments.

Job Description:
We are seeking a Senior Data Engineer to design and develop scalable data solutions using Java, GCP BigQuery, and Terraform. The role involves building data pipelines, managing large datasets, and implementing cloud infrastructure for data platforms.

Responsibilities include developing backend services in Java, designing and optimizing BigQuery data models, automating infrastructure deployment using Terraform, and ensuring high performance and reliability of data systems. The candidate will also collaborate with cross-functional teams to support analytics and reporting needs.

Must-Have Skills:

Strong experience with Java development

Hands-on expertise in GCP (BigQuery)

Experience with Terraform (Infrastructure as Code)

Strong SQL / BigQuery query optimization

Experience with Data Pipeline Development

Knowledge of Cloud Architecture &amp; Deployment

Skills:
Java, GCP, BigQuery, Terraform, SQL, Data Engineering</p> <!----></div></section> <section data-ev-sublocation="page" data-ev-label="contract_to_hire_tag_impression" data-ev-label-prefix="" impression="0.5" class="air3-card-section text-base-sm" data-v-ba9a22f4 data-v-3dd1f222><ul class="features list-unstyled m-0" data-v-ba9a22f4><!----> <!----> <li data-v-ba9a22f4><div data-cy="fixed-price" class="air3-icon md" data-v-ba9a22f4><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" role="img"><path vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke" stroke="var(--icon-color, #001e00)" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5" d="M13.17 3H21v7.83L10.83 21 3 13.17 13.17 3z"></path><path vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke" stroke="var(--icon-color, #001e00)" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5" d="M9.63 11.51a1.86 1.86 0 00.3 2.56 1.86 1.86 0 002.56.3 1.51 1.51 0 00.27-1.68c-.25-.54-.87-1.56-1.08-2.12A1.4 1.4 0 0112 9.12a1.84 1.84 0 012.55.31 1.84 1.84 0 01.33 2.57m-.31-2.57l.81-.81m-6.26 6.26l.81-.81m7.94-7.39a.55.55 0 100-1.1.55.55 0 000 1.1z"></path></svg></div> <div data-v-ba9a22f4><div data-v-2546b8da data-v-ba9a22f4><p class="m-0" data-v-2546b8da><strong data-v-2546b8da>
